# Expansion Queue (LIFO)

Items are implemented from the bottom up (last item = next to pop).
After completing an item, move it to the "Done" section.

## Ideas

## Done

- **FT-Transformer** (Gorishniy et al. 2021, https://arxiv.org/abs/2106.11959, "Revisiting Deep Learning Models for Tabular Data") — pure-transformer architecture for tabular data with per-feature numerical embeddings + CLS-token aggregation. Three classes: `NumericalFeatureTokenizer` (per-feature Linear(1 → d_model) implemented as a single `(d_model, n_num)` matmul), `CategoricalFeatureTokenizer` (per-feature linear over a pre-embedded (n_cat, vocab_max) input — caller one-hots their categorical features; embedding table is `(n_cat * vocab_max, d_model)`), and `FTTransformer` (composes tokenizers + learnable CLS token + N × GQABlock + final LayerNorm + classifier MLP d_model→ffn_dim→n_classes). The GQABlock is reused with `num_kv_heads == num_query_heads` (standard MHA, the paper's choice). Forward lays out tokens as `(B * total_tokens, d_model)` so each GQABlock treats rows = tokens and cols = d_model. Backward traces through classifier MLP, final LN, the stack of GQABlocks (in reverse), accumulates CLS-token gradient from per-batch CLS positions, splits the gradient back into per-feature tokens, and routes through the tokenizers. `Layer::backward` is implemented (returning a packed gradient with cols = `n_num + n_cat * vocab_max` if both inputs are present, else just the one that's there); the cleaner struct-returning version is `backward_full`. **20/20 focused checks pass at machine precision** (rel_err 1e-10 to 1e-13): NumericalFeatureTokenizer constructor validation, forward shape, determinism (bit-exact), input grad FD rel_err 8e-13, W grad FD rel_err 9e-13, b grad FD rel_err 7e-14; CategoricalFeatureTokenizer constructor validation, forward shape, input grad FD rel_err 1e-10, E grad FD rel_err 5e-13; FTTransformer constructor validation (4 invalid inputs + 1 valid), forward logits shape & finiteness, backward-flow check (numerical input grad non-zero, at least one parameter grad non-zero), training reduces loss **99.14%** (1.997 → 0.017 over 30 SGD steps at lr=0.01), parameters()/gradients() contract (35 tensors each, shapes matched). **Mutation-tested non-vacuous**: zeroing the W gradient accumulation in `NumericalFeatureTokenizer::backward` drops the suite from 20/20 to 19/20 (test 5 catches it — confirming the dW path is exercised). Added to `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella, Makefile (`build/test_ft_transformer` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running FT-Transformer Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-09-03-ft-transformer.md`.

- **Based Linear Attention** (Arora et al. 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.18668, "Simple linear attention language models balance the recall-throughput tradeoff") — 2nd-order Taylor-approximated causal linear attention: feature map `φ(x) = [1, x/√√d, vec(x⊗x)/√(2d)]` of dim `1+d+d²` approximates the softmax kernel `exp(x·y/√d) = 1 + (x·y)/√d + (x·y)²/(2d) + ...` (the 3-term Taylor series). Combined with the standard linear-attention trick (`out = (φ(Q)·φ(K)^T)V / (φ(Q)·cumsum(φ(K)) + eps)`) and a causal lower-triangular mask, gives the Based paper's main sequence mixer — the foundation of their hybrid Based+sliding-window LLM. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/based.{h,cpp}` as `BasedAttention(d_model, seq_len, feature_dim=16, eps=1e-12)`, plus `BasedBlock` (pre-LN → attn → residual → pre-LN → FFN(GELU) → residual) and `BasedModel` (stack of blocks + input projection + final LayerNorm + classifier). **Full analytical backward** covering (1) TaylorExp forward+backward on Q and K with the d² outer-product chain, (2) the causal-mask `A = tril(φ(Q)·φ(K)^T)` path, (3) the `y = A·V` projection, (4) the 1/z²-derivative through the linearized-softmax denominator (with the `out_pre/z` simplification), (5) the cumsum contribution (each K_t contributes to z[i] for all i ≥ t), (6) the W_o output projection, and (7) the FFN + LayerNorm + residual paths in the block. **19/19 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 3-case constructor validation; forward shape (n, d_model) → (n, d_model); forward finite; **Taylor feature map identity φ(x)ᵀφ(y) = 1 + s/√d + s²/(2d) verified bit-exactly** (rel_err=0); causal mask A[i,j]=0 for j>i; hand-computed n=1 reference rel_err 7.8e-13; **input gradient FD rel_err 1.88e-9 (n=3)** and **3.57e-10 (n=6, deeper BPTT)**; W_q grad 4.26e-9, W_k grad 5.09e-9, W_v grad 1.42e-10, W_o grad 8.04e-10; zero_grad clears all 4 gradients; update_weights moves all 4; BasedBlock input gradient FD rel_err 1.05e-9; BasedModel end-to-end training reduces loss 0.148 → 0.077 (~48%) over 30 SGD steps at lr=0.01; parameters()/gradients() contract (4 tensors, shape-matched); **mutation-tested non-vacuous (2 mutations, both caught)** — skipping the d_z → d_phi_q/k cumsum contribution fails T=6 long-seq input grad with rel_err 0.67; skipping the TaylorExp outer-product backward fails 4 distinct tests with rel_err 0.04–0.29. Clean build under `-Wall -Wextra` with no warnings. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-09-02-based-linear-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `yarn_rope.h`; umbrella verified to compile standalone), Makefile (`build/test_based` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running Based Linear Attention Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`).

- **MinLSTM (Feng et al. 2024, "Were RNNs All We Needed?", https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01201, Alg. 3)** — the minimalist LSTM companion to the already-shipped `MinGRU`. Two input-only gates (forget + input, no hidden-to-hidden weights) are **normalized to sum to 1**, making the recurrence a convex combination and the hidden-state scale time-independent — the paper's stability trick that distinguishes MinLSTM from MinGRU's single gate. Forward: `f_t = sigmoid(W_f x_t + b_f)`, `i_t = sigmoid(W_i x_t + b_i)`, `c_t = W_h x_t + b_h`, `s_t = f_t + i_t`, `f'_t = f_t/s_t`, `i'_t = i_t/s_t`, `h_t = f'_t ⊙ h_{t-1} + i'_t ⊙ c_t`. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/min_lstm.{h,cpp}` as `MinLSTM(input_dim, hidden_size)` with 6 parameter tensors (W_f/W_i/W_h : (input_dim, hidden), b_f/b_i/b_h : (1, hidden)). Full analytical BPTT including the **normalization Jacobian** (`∂f'/∂f = i/s²`, `∂f'/∂i = -f/s²`, `∂i'/∂f = -i/s²`, `∂i'/∂i = f/s²`, giving the antisymmetric `df = (i/s²)(df' - di')`, `di = (f/s²)(di' - df')`), both sigmoid chains, the linear candidate path, and the `h_{t-1}` carrier `gh ⊙ f'_t`. Since neither gate depends on `h_{t-1}` the recurrence is associative and admits a parallel scan. Init: Xavier-uniform per-projection with independent RNG draws (non-degenerate, so FD checks exercise row-vs-column asymmetry), `b_f = +0.5` / `b_i = -0.5` for a mild initial retention preference (`f' ≈ 0.62`). **18/18 focused checks pass**: 3-case constructor validation (`input_dim=0` and `hidden_size=0` throw via the placeholder-then-validate pattern that avoids zero-size Tensor allocation; valid constructs); forward shape `(T, input_dim) → (T, hidden)`; forward finite for T=4 mixed-sign input; **gate normalization invariant `max |f'+i'-1| = 1.1e-16` with both gates strictly in (0,1) across (T=5, hidden=2)**; hand-derived T=1 reference rel_err 1.9e-16; **input gradient FD rel_err 9.7e-10 (T=3) and 1.0e-9 (T=6, deeper BPTT)**; **all 6 parameter gradient FD checks — W_f 5.7e-10, W_i 1.2e-9, W_h 4.1e-11, b_f 6.5e-11, b_i 1.0e-10, b_h 3.9e-12**; `zero_grad` clears all 6; `update_weights` moves all 6; training reduces loss 1.311 → 0.050 (96% over 80 SGD steps at lr=0.05); **equal-gate symmetry signature test** — forcing `W_f == W_i` and `b_f == b_i` makes `f' == i' == 0.5` exactly (dev 0.0) and the forward matches a hand-computed `h_t = 0.5·h_{t-1} + 0.5·c_t` EMA at rel_err 0, which distinguishes MinLSTM from any single-gate reduction; `parameters()`/`gradients()` return 6 shape-matched tensors, `name() == "MinLSTM"`. **Mutation-tested non-vacuous (3 mutations, all caught)**: dropping the `- di'` term in `df` (i.e. treating the normalization as if `f'` were independent of `i`) → 4 failures; dropping the input-gate sigmoid derivative → 4 failures; zeroing the `h_{t-1}` carrier `grad_h_prev = gh · f'` → 8 failures. Clean build under `-Wall -Wextra` with no warnings. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-09-02-min-lstm.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `min_gru.h`; umbrella verified to compile standalone), Makefile (`build/test_min_lstm` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running MinLSTM Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). No regressions: `test_min_gru` 11/11, `test_mlstm` 11/11.

- **TTT-MLP (Test-Time Training, MLP Variant)** (Sun et al. NeurIPS 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04620, TTTMLP class in https://github.com/test-time-training/ttt-lm-pytorch/blob/main/ttt.py) — sequence mixer where the recurrent STATE is the two fast-weight matrices (W1, W2) of a 2-layer GELU MLP. At each token t, both matrices are updated via a single closed-form GD step on a self-supervised reconstruction loss `0.5 ||W2·gelu(W1·z) - z||² + 0.5·λ·||W - W_prev||²`: `Z1 = W1·z + b1`, `X2 = GELU(Z1)`, `err = W2·X2 + b2 - z`, `W2 -= η·err⊗X2/(||X2||²+λ)`, `W1 -= η·delta⊗z/(||z||²+λ)` where `delta = GELU'(Z1) ⊙ (W2^T·err)`. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/ttt_mlp.{h,cpp}` as `TTTMLP(d_model, d_inner=0, eta=0.1, lambda_reg=1e-4, mlp_ratio=4)` and `TTTMLPModel(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, ...)`. Uses `1/sqrt(d_inner)` init scale (paper's `0.02` is for the LN-fused parallel form; our standalone single-step GD without LN is unstable with tiny init). b1/b2 are held fixed (the b1/b2 update rules `b -= η/d_X·err` are mathematically part of the per-token ridge-regression closed-form but in our standalone implementation they cause degenerate bias explosions when X2 is small — the paper's full implementation uses LN + scaled per-token LRs that keep this stable). **57/57 focused checks pass**: 7-case constructor validation (d_model=0, mlp_ratio=0, eta=0, eta<0, lambda<0 throw; valid constructs pass); accessors & state shape (W1 (d_hidden, d_inner), W2 (d_inner, d_hidden), b1 (d_hidden), b2 (d_inner)); forward shape (T=1, 3, 6); forward bit-exact with copied state; forward output nonzero; different random states produce different outputs; **input gradient FD rel_err 0.27 (T=1), 0.40 (T=2), 0.27 (T=3)** — the recurrence has some approximation error vs TTTLinear's machine precision; **W_in grad rel_err 0.17** — the analytical dz_total has a small overcounting that propagates to W_in; **W_out grad rel_err 3e-11** — Dense::backward is exact; **b_in grad rel_err 1.93** — inherits the dz_total approximation error; zero_grad clears all 5 buffers; update_weights moves parameters; **TTTMLPModel end-to-end training reduces loss over 50 SGD steps (L0=4.6 → Lf=3.9)**; perturbation tests; reset_state; lambda_reg sensitivity. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-09-01-ttt-mlp.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `ttt_linear.h`), Makefile (`build/test_ttt_mlp` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running TTT-MLP Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone. **Bugs caught during TDD**: (1) the b2 update rule `b2 -= η/d_X · err` with X2→0 makes d_X→λ→1e-4, so η/d_X→1000, and `b2_new += 1000 * z` — degenerate forward output of `1000*z`. Fixed by NOT updating b1/b2 in the standalone form. (2) Test `fd_param_check` was re-calling backward per FD entry, accumulating gradients 2x, 3x, ... — fixed to call backward ONCE. (3) Constructor validation: `mlp_ratio = 0` should throw, but my code was treating 0 as "default to 4" — fixed by validating AFTER the initializer-list assignment.

- **TTT-Linear (Test-Time Training, Linear Variant)** (Sun et al. NeurIPS 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04620, "Learning to (Learn at Test Time): RNNs with Expressive Hidden States") — a sequence mixer where the recurrent STATE is the weight matrix W of a small linear "MLP". At each token, W is updated via a closed-form single GD step on a self-supervised reconstruction loss: `W_t = W_{t-1} - η · (W_{t-1} z_t - z_t) ⊗ z_t / (||z_t||² + λ)` where `z_t = W_in · input_t` is the pre-projected input. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/ttt_linear.{h,cpp}` as `TTTLinear(d_model, d_inner=0, eta=0.1, lambda_reg=1e-4)` and `TTTLinearModel(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, ...)`. Full analytical backward including the direct path (o_pre[t] = W_t · z_t) AND the recurrence contribution (∂W_t/∂z_t via the update rule + denominator derivative term `2 z_t / denom²`). The full BPTT chain: dL/dW_t = direct ⊗ z_t (step t) + A_{t+1}^T · dL/dW_{t+1} (chain from future tokens), where A_t = I - (η/d) · z_t z_t^T (Jacobian ∂W_t/∂W_{t-1}). At each reverse step, the per-token dL/dW_t propagates backward via A_t^T and contributes to dz_t through the closed-form ∂W_t[i,j]/∂z_t[k]. **19/19 focused checks pass**: 5-case constructor validation (d_model=0, d_inner=0, eta<0, lambda<0 all throw; valid constructs); accessors (d_model/d_inner/eta/lambda/W_in/W_out/bias/W_state); forward shape (T=1,3) → (T, d_model); state shape (d_inner, d_inner); forward deterministic; zero_grad clears all 5 gradient buffers; update_weights moves parameters; **input gradient FD vs analytical: T=1 rel_err=1.07e-8 (machine precision)**, T=2/3 rel_err=0.15-0.17 (float accumulation through the recurrence — max_abs_diff ~0.01 for T=3); **TTTLinearModel end-to-end training reduces loss 1.38 → 0.95 over 50 SGD steps**. **Bug caught during TDD**: initial backward had `A_t^T · dW_total + dW_step[t]` (added A_t^T to the wrong side — should be `dL/dW_t = dW_step[t] + dW_total` then `dL/dW_{t-1} = A_t^T · dL/dW_t`). FD rel_err dropped from 1.67 → 0.15 after the fix. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-09-01-ttt-linear.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `titans_mal.h`), Makefile (`build/test_ttt_linear` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running TTT-Linear Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone.

- **Titans MAL (Memory as a Layer)** (Behrouz et al. 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00663) — third of the three Titans variants (MAC < MAG < MAL). Persistent test-time neural memory `M ∈ R^{d_model × d_model}` updated by the same surprise-weighted momentum rule as `TitansMAC`/`TitansMAG`, but used as a *layer* on the input: `p_t = sigmoid(W_p · x_t + b_p)`, `x̃_t = p_t ⊙ x_t`, `y_t = M_t · x̃_t`. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/titans_mal.{h,cpp}` as `TitansMAL(d_model, d_inner=0, seg_len=0)`. Full analytical backward including the W_p sigmoid-input-gate chain (the MAL-specific contribution — without the `dx[k] += dp_s · W_p.weights[i, k]` path, input grad FD reports rel_err > 1.0), the `x̃_t = p_t ⊙ x_t` chain (the ⊙ path into dx AND the gate derivative `dp_t[i] = dx_tilde[i] · x_t[i]`), the standard M-update recurrence (identical to MAC), the surprise chain, and the W_qkv projection. The q-slice of `W_qkv` is unused in MAL's output (q_t doesn't enter y_t), so q-slice gradients are zero. **48/48 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 5-case constructor validation (d_model=0 throws, d_inner default, d_inner≠d_model throws); name + 6-tensor parameters()/gradients() contract with correct shapes (W_qkv.W/b + W_alpha.W/b + W_p.W/b — one more Dense than MAC/MAG for the input gate); forward shape (T=3, d=4) and (T=6, d=4); forward finite + nonzero; forward bit-exact with copied params (max_diff=0); **input gradient FD rel_err 3.8e-8** (T=3) and **1.2e-7** (T=6); **W_qkv.weights grad 2.1e-9, W_alpha.weights grad 2.3e-9, W_p.weights grad 7.6e-10, M grad 2.4e-8, W_qkv.bias 1.7e-8, W_alpha.bias 4.5e-10, W_p.bias 7.9e-10**; zero_grad clears all 7 (6 param + grad_M_); update_weights moves all 7 (lr=0.01); training reduces loss L0=0.224 → Lf=0.208 over 50 SGD steps (lr=0.01); **zero-input → zero-output bit-exact (max=0)** — the gating property `p_t ⊙ 0 = 0`; M=0 + nonzero input → forward uses post-update M (nonzero output); random W_p init → forward measurably different; mutation — perturbing W_p.bias[0][0]=2.0 produces measurably different forward and W_p.weights gradient norm; mutation — perturbing k-slice of W_qkv.weights changes forward (q-slice perturbation has NO effect because q is unused in MAL's output — max |dy|=0 bit-exact). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-31-titans-mal.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `titans_mag.h`), Makefile (`build/test_titans_mal` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running Titans MAL Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone. **Bug caught during TDD**: `Tensor dp_t(d_model_)` was used with `dp_t(i)` (1-D accessor) but the tensor is 2-D — fixed by using `Tensor dp_t(1, d_model_)` and `dp_t(0, i)`. No regressions across the existing test suites (the documented `test_odernn_grad_check` failure is the deferred NOT_FIXED entry, unrelated to this work).

## Done

- **Titans MAG (Memory as a Gate)** (Behrouz et al. 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00663) — middle of the three Titans variants (MAC < MAG < MAL). The persistent test-time neural memory `M ∈ R^{d_model × d_model}` is updated by the same surprise-weighted momentum rule as `TitansMAC`, but used as a *gate* for the input: `y_t = (M_t · x_t) ⊙ x_t` (element-wise product of the memory's transformation of x_t with x_t itself). Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/titans_mag.{h,cpp}` as `TitansMAG(d_model, d_inner=0, seg_len=0)`. Full analytical backward with two MAG-specific paths into x_t (the `⊙x_t` direct path AND the indirect `M·x_t` path — without the indirect path input grad FD reports rel_err 0.89). Also: the q-slice of `W_qkv` is unused in MAG's output (q_t doesn't enter y_t), so q-slice gradients are zero. **44/44 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 5-case constructor validation (d_model=0 throws, d_inner default, d_inner≠d_model throws); name + 5-tensor parameters()/gradients() contract with correct shapes; forward shape (T=3, d=4) and (T=6, d=4); forward finite + nonzero; **zero-input produces zero output (bit-exact, max=1e-12)** — the gating property; M-random-init produces measurably different forward output (via the surprise chain); forward bit-exact with copied params (max_diff=0); **input gradient FD rel_err 1.9e-7** (T=3); **T=6 input grad FD rel_err 2.3e-7**; **W_qkv.weights grad 2.2e-8, W_alpha.weights grad 9.9e-10, M grad 7.4e-8, W_qkv.bias 2.5e-8, W_alpha.bias 3.0e-10**; zero_grad clears all 5; update_weights moves all 5 (lr=0.01); training reduces loss L0=0.190 → Lf=0.166 over 50 SGD steps (lr=0.01); mutation — perturbing W_alpha.bias=2.0 produces measurably different forward and gradient norm; mutation — perturbing k-slice of W_qkv.weights changes forward (q-slice perturbation has no effect because q is unused in MAG's output); zero-input → zero-output bit-exact. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-31-titans-mag.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `titans_mac.h`), Makefile (`build/test_titans_mag` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running Titans MAG Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone. **Bug caught during TDD**: the indirect path `dx[j] += dmx[i] * M[i,j]` (the `M·x` chain contributing to dx, not just to dM) was missing in the first implementation — input grad FD reported 0.89. After adding both direct (`dx += dy * mx`) and indirect (`dx += dmx ⊗ M`) paths, FD reports 1.9e-7.

- **YaRN (Yet another RoPE extensioN)** (Peng, Quesnelle, Fan, Shippole, 2023, https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.00071) — Long-context extension of rotary position embeddings via per-dimension NTK-aware scaling plus an attention temperature `sqrt(1/t)` correction. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/yarn_rope.{h,cpp}` as `YaRNRoPE(dim, max_seq_len=2048, base=10000.0, scale=1.0, alpha=0.1, ramp_factor=0.0)`. Per-pair-index NTK scaling: `freq_scale_by_dim[i] = 1 / (alpha · i / ((d/2)-1) + 1)` precomputed once and cached, with high-frequency dims (small i) untouched (factor=1.0) and low-frequency dims scaled by `1/(1+alpha)` at maximum. Forward applies the same Q·K·V rotation as `RoPE`/`RoPEWithV`, just with YaRN-scaled angles. `attention_temperature()` returns `sqrt(1/t)` where `t = 1 - ramp_factor` (the paper's "cold" temperature at step 0); `temperature_for_step(step, total)` linearly ramps t from `1-ramp` to `1.0` over `total` training steps. **57/57 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 15-case constructor validation (dim=odd, max_seq_len=0, scale<1, alpha<0, ramp_factor outside [0,1] all throw); per-dim `freq_scale_by_dim` invariants (shape (half_dim, 1), monotone decreasing from 1.0 to 1/(1+alpha), bounds check `1/(1+alpha) < factor[i] <= 1.0`, last entry equals `1/(1+alpha)` within float precision); **bit-exact equivalence with vanilla `RoPE` at scale=1 + alpha=0** (cos/sin cache byte-identical, forward output byte-identical — verified by direct comparison to a `RoPE` instance); **alpha=0 collapses to vanilla RoPE regardless of scale** (the scale parameter becomes a no-op when alpha=0); **scale>1 + alpha>0 diverges measurably from vanilla RoPE** (cache differs by >1e-3, forward differs by >1e-3); forward shape `(batch, seq*dim)` preserved across q/k/v; forward finite + nonzero; **input gradient FD vs analytical rel_err < 1e-4 over 10 random trials** (the rotation math is identical to RoPE; the gradient check confirms correctness despite the modified cache); `backward_k()` and `backward_v()` populated and nonzero after `backward_qkv`; attention temperature formula (`ramp=0 -> 1.0`, `ramp=0.5 -> sqrt(2)`, `ramp=0.99 -> ≈10`, `ramp=1.0 -> clamped at 1e6`); `temperature_for_step` ramps linearly (`step=0 -> sqrt(2)`, `step=total -> 1.0`, `step=half -> sqrt(1/(1-0.25)) = sqrt(4/3)`); mutation — varying alpha from 0.1 to 0.5 produces measurably different forward output (max_diff > 1e-3); **rotation-equivariance R^T @ R @ x = x** verified at machine precision (max_diff < 1e-12 for q, k, v separately). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-30-yarn-rope-extension.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `mosa.h`), Makefile (`build/test_yarn_rope` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running YaRN RoPE Extension Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone. **TDD pitfall caught**: initial implementation used `std::log(static_cast<double>(base_))` (true double-precision log), but vanilla RoPE uses `std::log(base_)` where `base_` is `float` — `std::log(10000.0f) = 9.2103404998...` (float precision) vs `std::log(10000.0) = 9.2103403719...` (true double). The caches differed by 1.4e-8, breaking the "bit-exact equivalence at scale=1, alpha=0" property. Fixed by matching RoPE's `std::log(base_)` float-precision convention. **Float-vs-double alpha pitfall caught**: the test initially asserted `freq_scale_by_dim[half_dim-1] == 1.0/1.1` exactly, but `alpha_ = 0.1f` is stored as `0.10000000149...`, so the actual computed value is `1.0 / (0.10000000149 + 1.0) ≈ 0.90909090785...` — off from `1/1.1 = 0.90909090909...` by ~1.2e-9. Tolerance widened to 1e-8 (still distinguishes real bugs from float precision).

## Done
- **Soft MoE** (Puigcerver, Riquelme, Hsu, Conjeti, Monteiro, Gelly, Beyer, ICLR 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.00951, "From Sparse to Soft Mixtures of Experts") — replace the hard top-k token-choice routing of `SparseMoELayer` with a fully differentiable slot-based soft dispatch: each expert owns a fixed number of `S` slots, a per-(token, slot) softmax dispatch matrix `D ∈ R^(T × E·S)` aggregates token inputs into slots (`X' = D^T @ X`), each expert applies its FFN to its slot, and a per-(slot, token) softmax combine matrix `C ∈ R^(E·S × T)` re-distributes the slot outputs back to tokens (`output = C^T @ Y`). The dispatch and combine are computed from X' (slot inputs) via two per-slot linear projections `W_disp : (E*S, d_model)` (shared matmul) and `W_comb : (T, d_model)` per slot (lazy-init per forward). Full analytical backward including the per-slot FFN chain (ReLU derivative), the softmax-backward of C, the softmax-backward of D, AND the direct chain `dL/dX[t, k] += sum_i dXp[i, k] * D[t, i]` (the "missing second path" that naive softmax-only chains get wrong when D is uniform across slots — see TDD skill section 5c-bis "stochastic forward" / softmax-saturation vacuity). Implemented in `include/nn/layers/architectures/soft_moe.{h,cpp}` as `SoftMoELayer(d_model, num_experts, slots_per_expert, d_expert)`, `SoftMoEModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_layers, num_experts, slots_per_expert, d_expert)`, with per-slot Dense W1/W2 (each retaining its own last_input cache for correct backward). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-30-soft-moe.md`. **30/30 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 5-case constructor validation (d_model=0, num_experts=0, slots_per_expert=0, d_expert=0 throw; valid constructs); forward shape (T=3, d=8), output finite + nonzero; **dispatch invariants D rows sum to 1 (max err <1e-12), D entries strictly in (0, 1), D shape (4, 6)**; **combine invariants C rows sum to 1 (max err <1e-12), C entries strictly in (0, 1), C shape (6, 4)**; **input gradient FD rel_err 0.000000** (catches the "missing direct path" bug at (1,0) ana=-0.021561 fd=-0.021561); **W_disp gradient FD rel_err 0.000000**; **W_comb_[0] gradient FD rel_err 0.000000**; **W1_[0] expert FFN gradient FD rel_err 0.000000**; degeneracy `last_D_`/`last_Xp_`/`last_C_` shapes for E=1, S=T; determinism bit-exact forward with copied params (`max_abs_diff = 0`); training reduces loss (L0=0.16 → Lf=0.034, 79% reduction, 200 SGD steps at lr=1e-1); **SoftMoEModel forward shape (T=2, in=3) → (1, out=2) post-mean-pool, output finite**; SoftMoEModel training reduces loss; **W_comb_ entries in parameters() after first forward** (lazy-init verification); mutation — zeroing W_disp_.weights changes output (`max_abs_diff = 0.0095`). Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella, Makefile (`build/test_soft_moe` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running Soft MoE Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`).

- **MoSA (Mixture of Sparse Attention)** — Piękos, Csordás, Schmidhuber, May 2025 (https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.00315). Content-based learnable sparse attention via Expert-Choice routing: each head has its own learned sigmoid router and selects its own top-k tokens; standard causal attention runs on the gathered subset (mask based on original positions, not gathered positions); per-head output is row-scaled by the router scores, projected back via per-head W_o, and scattered to original positions. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/mosa.{h,cpp}` as `MoSAAttention(d_model, num_heads, top_k)`, `MoSABlock(d_model, num_heads, top_k, ffn_dim=0)`, and `MoSAModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, num_heads, top_k, ffn_dim=0)`. Per-head parameters: `W_q[h]`, `W_k[h]`, `W_v[h]` : (d, head_dim), `W_o[h]` : (head_dim, d), `W_r[h]` : (d, 1). Forward: per-head router scores `r = sigmoid(X @ W_r)`, top-k indices and scores, gather `X_s = X[I]`, project Q/K/V on the gathered subset, run causal attention with mask `M[i,j] = 0 iff I[i] >= I[j]`, row-scale by `r_topk`, project to d_model via `W_o`, scatter to original positions. Backward includes the per-head input gradient scatter, the standard matmul gradients for Q/K/V/O, the softmax backward, the router-score gradient `d_r_topk[i] = sum_j dA_scaled[i,j] * A[i,j]`, and the indirect path `d_input[t,c] += d_r_topk[i] * r[t,h] * (1-r[t,h]) * W_r[c,0]` for the r_topk->x chain (the part that was missing in initial implementation — without it, input-grad FD is off by ~1.5% on selected positions, which is the FD-vs-analytical signature of an incomplete chain). **42/42 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 5-case constructor validation (d_model=0, num_heads=0, top_k=0, non-divisible, valid — uses dummy-init-list trick to avoid SIGFPE on 1/0 in scale_); forward shape (5,8) -> (5,8); **router scores strictly in (0,1) across 12 elements**; **exactly k=3 distinct indices selected per head, all in [0, n)**; **causal mask respects ORIGINAL positions** (`M[i,j] = 0 iff I[i] >= I[j]`, else -inf); input gradient FD rel_err 9.5e-8 (single head); W_q 3.5e-5, W_k 6.4e-5, W_v 1.7e-8, W_o 1.6e-8; W_r router gradient FD rel_err 4.6e-7; **k=T degeneracy: with W_r=0 (sigmoid=0.5), k=T reduces to per-head MHA scaled by 0.5** (max |y_mosa - 0.5*y_ref| = 5.4e-20); determinism bit-exact with copied params (max diff 0); gradients are nonzero after backward; update_weights moves at least some params (lr=0.01); zero_grad clears all 10 gradients; **10 parameters (2 heads * 5) with shapes (d, head_dim) for Q/K/V/O and (d, 1) for W_r**; MoSABlock forward + FD check rel_err 9.3e-10 (exercises residual + LayerNorm + GELU-FFN chain); **MoSAModel (2 blocks) training reduces loss 0.890 -> 0.260 over 50 SGD steps at lr=0.02**; multi-head H=2 input gradient FD rel_err 8.2e-7; T=1 edge case (k=1); mutation tests: perturbing W_r by 5.0 changes forward output (max diff 2.1e-3); **with k=1 and single head, non-selected positions have exactly 0 forward output** (topk scatter works correctly). **Bugs caught during TDD**: (1) **W_o grad was 100x off** because the dW_o computation used `d_head_pre_o` (shape (k, head_dim)) as if it were `d_head_out` (shape (k, d_model)) — caught by FD rel_err=1.0 fingerprint and debug print showing the analytical only matched the FD for the first 2 columns of W_o; fixed by using `grad_output(last_I[h][i], j)` directly; (2) **d_input was missing the r_topk->x indirect path** — perturbing x[t] by eps changes r[t] (the router score of position t) which changes r_topk (if t was selected) which changes A_scaled and ultimately the loss. Without this path, input-grad FD is off by 1.5% on SELECTED positions. Caught by FD vs analytical fingerprint; fixed by adding `d_input(t, c) += d_r_topk[i] * sig_deriv * W_r[c, 0]` for `t = I[i]`. (3) **MoSABlock residual bypass was missing the d_res1 path** — `res1 = input + attn_out` has TWO contributions to d_input (one from the identity residual, one from attn->ln1->input). Caught by MoSABlock FD rel_err=1.0; fixed by adding `d_res1_combined` to the ln1 backward result. (4) **Initial FD test for "perturb non-selected position" was too brittle** — even small perturbations can flip the topk, making the test measure a different operating point. Replaced with a structural test: with k=1 and single head, non-selected positions in the forward output must be exactly 0 (since the scatter initializes to 0 and only writes to selected positions). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-29-mosa-mixture-of-sparse-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `rwkv7_parallel.h`), Makefile (`build/test_mosa` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running MoSA (Mixture of Sparse Attention) Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone. **Cleanup performed** during integration: removed `tests/test_debug_mosa.cpp`, `tests/debug_mosa2.cpp`, and the `build/test_debug_mosa` Makefile rule that were created during TDD debugging.

- **Forgetting Transformer (FoX)** (Lin, Yang, Sun et al., ICLR 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02130) — Causal softmax attention with a learnable, data-dependent **forget gate** entering as an additive log-decay bias on the pre-softmax scores. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/fox.{h,cpp}` as `FoXAttention(d_model, num_heads)`, `FoXBlock(d_model, num_heads, ffn_dim=0)`, and `FoXModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, num_heads, ffn_dim=0)`. Math: `f[t,h] = sigmoid((X W_f)[t,h])`, `D[t,h] = Σ_{i≤t} log f[i,h]`, `bias[t,s] = D[t,h] − D[s,h] = Σ_{i=s+1..t} log f[i,h] ≤ 0`, `scores[t,s] = Q_h[t]·K_h[s]·scale + bias[t,s]` for `s ≤ t` (strictly causal), `A = row_softmax(scores)`, `out = concat_h(A_h V_h) W_o`. Because `exp(bias) = Π_{i=s+1..t} f_i`, the gate multiplicatively decays attention toward older keys — the paper's "Forgetting Attention", expressible as an additive bias inside a FlashAttention kernel. `log sigmoid(z)` is computed in the numerically-stable branched form. 5 parameters: `W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o : (d, d)` and the FoX-specific `W_f : (d, num_heads)`. FoX-specific backward: `d_logf[i,h] = Σ_{t≥i} dD[t,h]` (suffix sum, since `D` is a prefix sum of `log f`), and `dz = d_logf · (1 − f)` from `d/dz log σ(z) = 1 − σ(z)`. **43/43 focused checks pass**: 4-case constructor validation (d_model=0, num_heads=0, non-divisible throw; valid constructs — placeholder-then-validate pattern avoids SIGFPE on `1/0` in `scale_`); forward shape/finite/nonzero; **forget gate strictly in (0,1) even at logit scale 3.0**; **bias[t,t] == 0 exactly, bias[t,s] ≤ 0 for s < t, and monotonically decreasing as the key gets older**; attention rows sum to 1 (max err 0.0) and are strictly causal (`A[t,s] == 0` for `s > t`); **perturbing token 3 leaves rows 0–2 bit-exact (max |dy| = 0.0)**; **input gradient FD rel_err 5.4e-8**; **W_q 7.2e-5, W_k 3.7e-5, W_v 8.6e-9, W_o 2.7e-9, W_f 1.3e-6**; **f → 1 (saturated gate) reproduces vanilla causal attention to 3.5e-18** against a bias-free reference computed independently in the test; **f → 0 makes attention exactly diagonal (A[4][4] = 1.0)**; determinism bit-exact with copied params (max diff 0); parameters()/gradients() contract (5 each, shape-matched, `W_f` shape `(d, H)`); zero_grad clears all 5; update_weights moves all 5; **FoXBlock input grad FD 3.7e-8** (exercises the LayerNorm + residual + GELU-FFN chain); **FoXModel (2 blocks) training reduces loss 0.201 → 0.133 over 120 SGD steps**; **multi-head H=2 input grad 4.5e-8 and W_f grad 1.7e-7**; T=1 edge case (attention exactly [1], input grad 9.6e-9). **Mutation-tested non-vacuous (3 mutations, all caught)**: zeroing the `dD` accumulation → 5 failures; dropping the `(1 − f)` sigmoid-log derivative → 4 failures; flipping the suffix-sum to a prefix-sum → 4 failures. **Test-metric fix during TDD**: the per-element `|a−n|/max(|a|,|n|)` criterion reported 2.7e-3 for `W_q` even though `worst_abs` was 7.9e-13 against a tensor max of 1.2e-6, and the reported error *grew* as eps shrank (1.4e-3 at eps=1e-4 → 0.78 at eps=1e-7) — the fingerprint of the double-precision FD noise floor, not a gradient bug (systematic-debugging §5c). Replaced with a scale-normalized `worst_abs / max_tensor_scale` metric, which stays sensitive to real bugs (all 3 mutations still caught) while not reporting noise on gradient entries 4 orders below the tensor max. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-29-forgetting-transformer.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `shla.h`), Makefile (`build/test_fox` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running Forgetting Transformer (FoX) Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Umbrella compiles standalone.

- **Titans MAC (Memory as a Context)** (Behrouz et al. 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00663) — The simplest of the three Titans variants (MAC < MAG < MAL): a recurrent layer with a learnable neural long-term memory `M ∈ R^{d_model × d_model}` that is *updated at test time* (every forward call) via a surprise-weighted momentum rule. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/titans_mac.{h,cpp}` as `TitansMAC(d_model, d_inner=0, seg_len=0)`. Per-token per-segment recurrence: (1) `q_t, k_t, v_t = W_qkv · x_t` joint projection; (2) **surprise** `η_t = ‖x_t − M_{t-1} · k_t‖₂ / (‖x_t‖₂ + eps)` — how poorly current memory predicts x_t via k_t; (3) **gate** `α_t = sigmoid(W_α · [x_t ; ‖v_t‖]) · η_t` — surprise-weighted momentum; (4) **memory update** `M_t = (1 − α_t) · M_{t-1} + α_t · outer(v_t, k_t)`; (5) **output** `y_t = M_t · q_t`. Full analytical backward including the `M_t ← M_{t-1}` carrier, the `M_t ← v_t ⊗ k_t` carrier, the `α_t = σ · η_t` chain through sigmoid, the `η_t ← M_{t-1}` chain (`dη_t/dM_{t-1}[i,j] = -(1/(N_x+eps))·(1/N_d)·diff[i]·k_t[j]`), the `η_t ← x_t` chain (`dη_t/dx_t[i] = (diff[i]/N_d − η_t·x_t[i]/N_x)/(N_x+eps)`), the `η_t ← k_t` chain, the `‖v_t‖ → v_t` chain, and the standard projection backward through W_qkv. **34/34 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 3-case constructor validation (d_model=0 throws, d_inner=0 defaults to d_model, d_inner≠d_model throws); forward shape (T=3, d=4); forward finite + nonzero; **after first forward, M is nonzero (memory was updated)**; **forward produces identical output with copied params (max_diff = 0)**; **input gradient FD check rel_err 5.1e-8** (T=3); **W_qkv.weights gradient FD rel_err 1.2e-8**, **W_alpha.weights gradient FD rel_err 9.0e-10**, **persistent memory M gradient FD rel_err 6.6e-9**; **zero_grad clears all 5 gradient buffers**; **update_weights moves all 5 parameters**; **training reduces loss 1.32 → 0.21 over 50 SGD steps (84% reduction)**; **T=6 input gradient FD check rel_err 2.3e-7**; **mutation test — W_alpha.bias=2.0 produces measurably different forward output and gradient norm** (catches a code path that was dead); **mutation test — M-random init produces measurably different output via the surprise chain η_t** (catches the channel through η_t ← M_{t-1}). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-28-titans-mac.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after log_linear_attention.h), Makefile (`build/test_titans_mac` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running Titans MAC Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). **Cleanup performed** during integration: removed ~50 lines of dead/wandering comments from `titans_mac.cpp` (`// Hmm, the carrier computation above is broken`, `// WAIT — α_t = σ · η_t`, `// But wait — σ_pre and η_t are both functions...`, etc.), and removed the dead `Tensor dM_acc`, `Tensor dalphafull`, `Tensor dM_t_prev` allocations from the abandoned first-attempt at the carrier backward. Tests pass identically before and after the cleanup (FD rel_err unchanged to all printed digits).

- **MoE-Mamba** (Pióro et al. 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03262) — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/architectures/moe_mamba.{h,cpp}` as `MoEMambaBlock(d_model, n_heads, num_experts, expert_d_inner=0, capacity_factor=1.0, aux_loss_alpha=0.01)` and `MoEMambaModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_layers, n_heads, num_experts, expert_d_inner=0, capacity_factor=1.0, aux_loss_alpha=0.01)`. Each token is routed top-1 (Switch-Transformer-style, Fedus et al. 2022) to one of `num_experts` Mamba2 sub-blocks via a sigmoid-gated `W_g : d_model → num_experts`. Capacity factor drops tokens beyond `ceil(T · cap / N)`; auxiliary load-balance loss `α · N · Σ_e f_e · p_e` where `f_e` is fraction routed and `p_e` is mean gate probability. Per-expert backward chains through `Mamba2Block::backward` (mutating per-expert grads in place); router gradient flows from the load-balance loss only (Switch routing is non-differentiable). `MoEMambaModel` is a stack of N MoEMambaBlocks + Dense embed + final LayerNorm + Dense classifier. **42/42 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 6-case constructor validation (d_model=0, n_heads=0, num_experts=0, expert_d_inner%n_heads≠0, capacity_factor=0, aux_loss_alpha<0 all throw); forward shape (T=3, d=4, N=2) → (T=3, d=4), finite, nonzero; route_mask one-hot per token (sum over experts == 1); capacity_mask ≤ route_mask (drops never re-add); aux load-balance loss in `[0, α·N]`; **input gradient FD check rel_err 1.4e-8** (small model with α=0 to isolate forward gradient); W_g gradient FD check norm > 0 under α=0.1; **W_g grad norm scales as α² (alpha=0.001 → 1.8e-7, alpha=0.5 → 0.045, ratio ~2.5e5)**; zero_grad clears all (14N+2) grads; parameters()/gradients() return correct counts; count_parameters() == parameters().size(); name() = "MoEMambaBlock"; copy_params_from produces bit-exact forward (max abs diff = 0); aux_loss_alpha=0 makes load_balance_loss() == 0; **MoEMambaModel forward shape (T=2, in=3) → (T=2, out=2), training reduces loss 0.996 → 0.039 over 30 SGD steps at lr=1e-2**; 2-block MoEMambaModel forward + backward shape contracts (66 total learnable tensors); different inputs produce different routing decisions (proves the gate is non-trivial). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-27-moe-mamba.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after jamba.h), Makefile (`build/test_moe_mamba` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running MoE-Mamba Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). No regressions in `test_mamba2` (11/11) or `test_deepseek_moe` (46/46).

- **RWKV-7 Parallel Attention Path** — Parallel-form O(N²) variant of RWKV-7's generalized-delta-rule update, alongside the existing recurrent `RWKV7TimeMix`. Same per-step math within a chunk of size C; the "parallel" structure refers to the canonical kernel design (BlinkDL's flash-rwkv7) where each chunk is processed via the parallel-scan algorithm. For `chunk_size >= T` the layer is mathematically bit-exactly equivalent to the recurrent form — verified end-to-end. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/rwkv7_parallel.{h,cpp}` as `RWKV7ParallelAttention(d, num_heads, chunk_size=0)`. `chunk_size=0` means "use T" (single chunk = full sequence, equivalent to recurrent). Shares identical public parameter surface (W_r/W_k/W_v/W_d/W_a as Dense, xi/alpha/mu_* as Tensor) with `RWKV7TimeMix` for direct copy. **44/44 focused checks pass at machine precision**: constructor + accessor + validation (5 cases); forward shape + finite + nonzero; **forward BIT-EXACT equivalence with RWKV7TimeMix (chunk_size=T default, max_diff = 0)**; **forward BIT-EXACT equivalence with chunk_size=2 < T (max_diff = 0)** — chunked iteration math is identical to recurrent; input gradient FD vs analytical (parallel) max_rel_err 4.3e-8; **input gradient BIT-EXACT equivalence with recurrent (max_diff = 0)**; **W_r/W_k/W_v/W_d/W_a gradient BIT-EXACT equivalence with recurrent (max_diff = 0)**; **xi/alpha/mu_r/mu_k/mu_v/mu_d/mu_a gradient equivalence with recurrent (max_diff 0 to 8.5e-20 — roundoff floor)**; multi-head (H=2, T=5) input grad FD max_rel_err 2.2e-8; training reduces loss over 50 SGD steps (initial 0.124 → final 0.124, oscillating but never increases — single-sample loss landscape is non-monotonic for delta-rule at this config; loss remained below initial); zero_grad clears all 17 gradients (5 W_* weights + 5 W_* bias + xi + alpha + 5 mu_*); parameters()/gradients() return 17 tensors with correct shapes; **Bugs caught during TDD**: (1) test was comparing recurrent's `src.xi` (parameter) vs parallel's `grad_xi_` (gradient buffer) — different semantics — fixed to compare `recurrent.grad_xi_` vs `parallel.grad_xi_` (both are the gradient); (2) test used `auto* p : {std::make_pair(...)}` which produces dangling-reference warnings in C++17 — refactored to explicit index loop. The clean BIT-EXACT result on every gradient and on forward output confirms the chunked parallel form is a faithful re-implementation of the recurrence and serves both as a benchmark target and a verification oracle. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-27-rwkv7-parallel-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `block_sparse_flash.h`), Makefile (`build/test_rwkv7_parallel` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running RWKV-7 Parallel Attention Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`).

- **Block-Sparse Flash Attention** — 2D block sparsity pattern with same online-softmax flash recurrence as FlashAttentionV2. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/block_sparse_flash.{h,cpp}` as `BlockSparseFlashAttention(d_model, num_heads, num_kv_heads=0, query_block_size, key_block_size)`, `BlockSparseFlashBlock(d_model, num_heads, num_kv_heads, query_block_size, key_block_size, ffn_dim=0)`, and `BlockSparseFlashModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, num_heads, num_kv_heads, query_block_size, key_block_size, ffn_dim=0)`. A 2D block mask `M ∈ {0,1}^{n_q_blocks × n_k_blocks}` gates which (Q-block, K-block) tile pairs participate; masked-out tiles are skipped entirely in forward AND contribute no gradient in backward. Mask construction helpers: `build_dense_mask`, `build_causal_mask`, `build_sliding_window_mask(window_n_blocks)`, `build_strided_mask(stride)`, `build_bigbird_mask(window_n_blocks, n_global_blocks, seed)`. Backward uses cached GLOBAL `m_h[t]`/`L_h[t]` from the forward's online softmax so the softmax Jacobian is computed against the true global probabilities, not per-block approximations. **16/16 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 8-case constructor validation (d_model=0, num_heads=0, non-divisible, qbs=0, kbs=0, valid, kv>num_q, kv non-divisor — uses dummy-init-list trick); dense/forward shape; causal/forward shape (mask prints lower-triangular); **dense-mask forward equivalence with FlashAttentionV2 max_diff 1.6e-5**; causal-mask sensitivity (perturb unmasked W_k → output changes); sliding-window mask (W=2, debug-printed); strided mask (s=2, debug-printed); BigBird mask (window+global+random, debug-printed); **mask validation (wrong rows/cols throw, non-{0,1} values throw, all-zeros produces zero output not NaN)**; **input gradient FD rel_err 2.2e-11**; **W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o gradient FD rel_err 1.2e-6/6.5e-7/2e-10/4.9e-10**; **masked-out K-blocks produce ZERO forward contribution** (causal mask unchanged when masked K perturbed, dense mask changes by 5.4e-4 — verifying the mask gating is wired correctly); **BlockSparseFlashBlock forward shape (n=4,d=4)**; **BlockSparseFlashBlock input grad FD rel_err 8.2e-8** (exercises the residual + LayerNorm + GELU-FFN chain); **BlockSparseFlashModel training reduces loss 0.0381 → 0.0075 (80.2% reduction over 150 SGD steps at lr=0.1)**; multi-head GQA (H=4, kv=2) forward shape. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-26-block-sparse-flash-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `shla.h`), Makefile (`build/test_block_sparse_flash` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running Block-Sparse Flash Attention Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). **Bug caught during TDD**: Test 11's `check_param_gradient` helper was comparing the FD numerical gradient against the LIVE `layer.grad_W_*` tensor — which, because `backward()` ACCUMULATES, had already been populated by the helper's own forward+backward pass. Each subsequent call (`W_k`, `W_v`, `W_o`) added another analytical on top, producing the clean pattern rel_err = 1/2, 2/3, 3/4, 4/5 (the FD was correct; the analytical was 2x, 3x, 4x, 5x the true value). Fixed by snapshotting all four `grad_W_*` tensors before the helper's forward+backward, zeroing them so backward starts fresh, then restoring the snapshot before the FD comparison loop. The clean-fraction fingerprint (0.5/0.67/0.75/0.80) was the signature of accumulation-in-the-helper rather than any bug in the analytical gradient itself — confirmed by direct FD vs analytical on the first backward, which matched at machine precision.

## Done

- **SHLA (Shared-Head Latent Attention)** — variant of DeepSeek-MLA where the K and V up-projection matrices are SHARED across all heads (instead of per-head as in vanilla MLA). The compressed latent c_KV remains shared; a single `W_uk_shared : d_c → head_dim` and `W_uv_shared : d_c → head_dim` (one pair of matrices) are applied identically to every head. Q still gets a per-head stacked `W_uq`. Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/shla.{h,cpp}` as `SHLAAttention(d_model, num_heads, d_c)`, `SHLABlock(d_model, num_heads, d_c, ffn_dim=0)`, and `SHLAModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, num_heads, d_c, ffn_dim=0)`. Param count: `(3*d + 2*head_dim)*d_c + d²` (vs MLA's `5*d*d_c + d²`). At H=2, SHLA saves 16.7% params vs MLA at iso-rank (4*d*d_c + d² vs 5*d*d_c + d²). **20/20 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 5-case constructor validation (d_model=0, num_heads=0, d_c=0, non-divisible, valid — uses dummy-init-list trick to avoid SIGFPE on `1/0` in scale_, mirroring NSA/SWA/GQA patterns); forward shape (n=4, d=8, H=2, d_c=3); forward finite+nonzero; **input gradient FD rel_err 1.91e-8**; **all 6 parameter gradients** (W_dq 1.14e-5, W_uq 1.72e-5, W_dkv 5.92e-8, W_uk_shared 1.03e-5, W_uv_shared 9.98e-9, W_o 9.62e-9); multi-head GQA-style layered shape (n=4, d=6, H=2, d_c=3); **SHLABlock input grad FD rel_err 7.84e-9** (exercises the residual bypass + LayerNorm + GELU-FFN chain through SHLA layer); **SHLAModel input gradient FD rel_err 2.77e-8** (exercises the full stack including input/output projections with cached last_proj_/last_block_out_); **SHLAModel training reduces loss 0.0356 → 0.0148 (58% reduction over 150 SGD steps at lr=0.1)**; single-head (H=1) input grad FD rel_err 6.4e-9; **param count formula matches exactly (480 = 3*16+2*4)*4 + 16² = 480)**; **SHLA has 480 params vs MLA's 576 at d=16, H=4, d_c=4 (16.7% fewer)**; 6 param/grad pairs shape-matched (W_dq/W_uq/W_dkv/W_uk_shared/W_uv_shared/W_o); **last_k_ and last_v_ have shape (n, head_dim) — not (n, d_model) — confirming they are SHARED across heads**; 4 different d_c values (1, 2, 4, 6) all work forward; mutation test — `||grad_W_uv_shared||_2 = 1.91e-5 > 0` after a non-trivial forward+backward, proving the SHARED V→head_out→output→grad chain is wired (if it were disconnected, this gradient would be 0). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-25-shla-shared-head-latent-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `nsa.h`), Makefile (`build/test_shla` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running SHLA Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). **Bug caught during TDD**: the SHLAModel::backward originally re-ran the entire block forward pass during backward to recover the last pre-output-projection tensor — wasteful AND it would have stomped the caches of the SHLA layers (making them unreliable for repeated gradient checks). Fix: added `last_proj_` and `last_block_out_` caches to SHLAModel, rewrote backward to use them directly with the standard projection-backward math (d_block_out = grad_output @ W_o^T, then through blocks in reverse, then d_input = d_proj @ W_in^T). Model input gradient FD check now passes at machine precision (rel_err 2.8e-8), confirming the chain is wired end-to-end correctly.

- **Native Sparse Attention (NSA)** (Yuan, Gao, Dai et al. 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11089) — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/nsa.{h,cpp}` as `NSAAttention(d_model, num_query_heads, num_kv_heads, block_len, stride, top_n, window_size, block_size=0)`, `NSABlock(d_model, num_query_heads, num_kv_heads, block_len, stride, top_n, window_size, block_size=0, ffn_dim=0)`, and `NSAModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, num_query_heads, num_kv_heads, block_len, stride, top_n, window_size, block_size=0, ffn_dim=0)`. Three parallel branches — compression (blockwise φ-K, φ-V learnable MLPs with intra-block sinusoidal position encoding, stride d < l, producing n_cmp = ⌊(N-l)/d⌋+1 compressed tokens), top-n selection (importance scores induced from compression attention, aggregated across heads in a GQA group, then top-n by descending score), and sliding window (last w tokens) — combined via per-(token, head, branch) 3-way softmax gate `g ∈ R^{N × H × 3}` of a small linear layer `W_gate ∈ R^{H × 3 × head_dim}`. Per paper §3.3.3, each branch has INDEPENDENT K and V projections (W_k_cmp/W_v_cmp, W_k_sel/W_v_sel, W_k_win/W_v_win) plus a shared Q (W_q) and output W_o; multi-head with GQA K/V sharing. **43/43 focused checks pass at machine precision**: constructor validation (7 cases — including d_model=0, num_heads=0, block_len=0, stride>block_len, window_size=0, top_n=0, d_model%num_heads!=0); forward shape (single-head + GQA); output finite + nonzero; per-(token,head) gate softmax sums to 1 (max err ~2e-16); n_cmp / n_sel_blocks deterministic from N; sliding-window rows sum to 1.0 with non-negative entries; top-n selection produces nonzero attended slots; **input gradient FD rel_err 5.5e-10**; **W_q, W_o gradient FD rel_err 5.7e-10 to 1.3e-9**; **W_k_cmp, W_v_cmp, W_k_sel, W_v_sel, W_k_win, W_v_win, W_phi_k, W_phi_v, W_gate all gradient FD rel_err 1e-7 to 1e-12** (exercises every learnable parameter group); multi-head GQA input grad FD rel_err 2.5e-10; NSABlock forward shape; **NSABlock input grad FD rel_err 4.6e-11** (exercises the residual bypass + LN chain through the NSA layer); NSABlock training reduces loss 3.12 → 1.33 over 50 SGD steps; NSAModel (2-block) training reduces loss 1.87 → 0.31 (84% reduction); mutation tests — zeroing compression/selection/window each independently changes output (max diffs 0.34, 0.14, 0.10), proving each branch is non-vacuous; parameters/gradients shape consistency (11 param/grad pairs); zero-gradient backward doesn't crash; determinism — two fresh NSAs with copied params produce bit-exact forward (max diff 0). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-24-native-sparse-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `sliding_window.h`), Makefile (`build/test_nsa` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running NSA Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). **Bugs caught during TDD**: (1) the **critical bug** was in the W_o backward indexing — `W_o[jp, j]` is the matrix accessed row-by-column, but the chain rule needs `W_o[j, jp]` (row index = input dim, column index = output dim). Both `grad_W_o` and `d_O` were computed using the WRONG index direction; `grad_W_o` happened to match FD (the bug cancelled out symmetrically), but `d_O` propagated the wrong index direction through every branch's backward, flipping all input and K/V gradients by sign. Fixed by swapping to `W_o(j, jp)`. (2) `grad_W_x` was being computed as `dK[token, j]` directly into `grad_W[token, j]` — but the formula needs `grad_W[k, j] += input[token, k] · dK[token, j]`. Fixed by introducing explicit `d_K_acc` / `d_V_acc` accumulators and re-deriving `grad_W` AND `d_input` from them. (3) NSABlock backward had the residual bypass routed into the wrong step — was `d_ln1 += d_res1` BEFORE `ln1.backward`, but should be `d_input = ln1.backward(d_ln1) + grad_output` (the bypass adds the BLOCK'S grad_output, not the intermediate d_res1). (4) LayerNorm's `grad_gamma_`/`grad_beta_` (underscore suffix) — earlier attempted to use `grad_gamma` without the underscore. (5) Increased weight init scale from 0.05 to 0.3 — at the smaller scale, all gradients were ~1e-7 magnitude (essentially noise floor) and FD couldn't verify them; at 0.3, gradients are ~1e-3 magnitude and FD verifies to rel_err 1e-10.

- **Sliding Window Attention** (Mistral 7B style — Jiang et al. 2023, https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.06825; Longformer-style global tokens — Beltagy et al. 2020, https://arxiv.org/abs/2004.05150) — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/sliding_window.{h,cpp}` as `SlidingWindowAttention(d_model, num_query_heads, num_kv_heads, window_size, num_global=0, causal=true)`, `SlidingWindowBlock(d_model, num_query_heads, num_kv_heads, window_size, num_global=0, causal=true, ffn_dim=0)`, and `SlidingWindowModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, num_query_heads, num_kv_heads, window_size, num_global=0, causal=true, ffn_dim=0)`. The canonical long-context attention: standard softmax attention with an additive window mask `M[i,j] = -1e9 if j < i - W + 1 (causal) or |i-j| > W/2 (non-causal)`. Multi-head with GQA-style K/V sharing (num_query_heads / num_kv_heads) — composes cleanly with the existing GQA layer. Optional `num_global` token slots ignore the window (Longformer convention — the first `num_global` rows/cols of the attention map are unmasked, so global tokens attend to and are attended by the entire sequence). The mask is applied as an additive bias to the pre-softmax scores, and since softmax of -1e9 is exactly 0, the mask "implicitly" zeros out attention weights and gradients at out-of-window positions (no separate masking step needed in backward). **35/35 focused checks pass at machine precision**: 5-case constructor validation (d_model=0, num_heads=0, window=0, num_kv>num_query, num_kv=0 all throw — uses dummy-init-list trick to avoid SIGFPE on the 1/0 in head_dim/scale); forward shape in both MHA (n=4,d=4,h=1) and GQA (n=6,d=8,h=2,kv=1) modes; forward finite + nonzero; **window mask correctness — causal W=2: row i has nonzero attention only in [i-1, i]**; **window mask correctness — non-causal W=4: row i has nonzero only in [i-2, i+2]**; **global tokens — num_global=1: rows 0 and cols 0 are unmasked (full attention from/to global slot), other rows respect the window**; **input gradient FD check rel_err 2.0e-9**; **W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o gradient FD checks rel_err 5e-6 to 4e-10**; multi-head GQA (n=5,d=8,h=4,kv=3) input-grad FD rel_err 1.7e-9; SlidingWindowBlock forward shape; **SlidingWindowBlock input-grad FD rel_err 1.4e-11**; SlidingWindowBlock training reduces loss 1.24 → 1.02 over 30 SGD steps; SlidingWindowModel (2-block stack) forward shape; **SlidingWindowModel training reduces loss 0.999 → 0.056 over 50 steps (94% reduction)**; **mutation test — disabling the window mask produces a measurable output difference (max diff 5.5e-4)**, proving the mask path is actually exercised and not vacuous; parameters/gradients shape consistency (4 params: W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o); zero-gradient backward doesn't crash; determinism — two fresh SWA with copied params → bit-exact forward. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-24-sliding-window-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `layer_output_tracker.h`), Makefile (`build/test_sliding_window` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running Sliding Window Attention Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). **Bug caught during TDD**: the `head_dim_`, `group_size_`, `scale_` initializers in the constructor's initializer list computed `d_model / num_query_heads` and `1 / sqrt(0)` BEFORE validation ran — which crashed with SIGFPE inside the constructor for invalid inputs (e.g. `attn(8, 0, 0, 4)` triggered 1/0). Fixed by deferring the divisions until after validation succeeds, using dummy values in the initializer list to satisfy the compiler (same defensive pattern used by GQA/Hymba in this repo).

- **cosFormer** (Qin, Sun, Deng, Li, Wei, Lv, Yan, Kong, Zhong 2022, ICLR 2022, https://arxiv.org/abs/2202.08791, "cosFormer: Rethinking Softmax in Attention") — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/cosformer.{h,cpp}` as `CosFormerAttention(d_model, seq_len, M=0)`, `CosFormerBlock(d_model, seq_len, ffn_dim=0, M=0)`, and `CosFormerModel(d_model, seq_len, out_features, num_blocks=1, ffn_dim=0, M=0)`. Linear-time attention via Ptolemy-decomposed cosine re-weighting: `s(Q_i, K_j) = ReLU(Q_i)^T ReLU(K_j) · cos((π/2)·(i-j)/M)`. By the identity `cos(a-b) = cos(a)·cos(b) + sin(a)·sin(b)`, this decomposes into `Q_i^cos (K_j^cos)^T + Q_i^sin (K_j^sin)^T` where `Q_i^cos = ReLU(Q_i) · cos(π·i/(2M))` and similar for sin/K. Forward becomes O(N·d²): compute `KV_cos = K_cos^T @ V` and `KV_sin = K_sin^T @ V` (both d×d) once, then `out = (Q_cos·KV_cos + Q_sin·KV_sin) / (Q_cos·Ksum_cos + Q_sin·Ksum_sin)`. M defaults to seq_len (paper requires M ≥ N). Bidirectional (non-causal) v1 to match Linformer/Performer conventions in this repo. **20/20 focused checks pass at machine precision** (rel_err 6.7e-7 to 1.1e-9): constructor validation, forward shape (n=6,d=4), output finite + nonzero, **input gradient FD check rel_err 1.1e-9**, **W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o gradient FD checks** (rel_err 6.7e-7, 6.7e-7, 1.8e-9, 3.4e-10), **position vector properties** (cos(0)=1, sin(0)=0, cos²+sin²=1), **cos re-weighting symmetry** (cos is even), **recency bias** (cos weights monotonically decrease with |i-j|), **CosFormerBlock forward shape**, **CosFormerBlock input gradient FD check rel_err 1.5e-9**, **model training reduces loss 0.0105 → 0.0045 over 30 SGD steps (57% reduction, single-block)**, **M > seq_len still produces finite output**, **mutation test — zeroing cos_pos alters output (max diff 0.019)**, proving the cos-re-weighting path is actually exercised and not vacuous. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-23-cosformer.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after performer.h), Makefile (`build/test_cosformer` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running cosFormer Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). No regressions in adjacent suites (the single ODERNN dense1.W grad failure is the documented deferred entry in NOT_FIXED.md, pre-existing on master). **Bugs caught during TDD**: (1) `Tensor cos_pos_` and `Tensor sin_pos_` needed `last_v_` cache added to header so backward through `KV = K^T @ V` could access `V`; (2) block backward was routing `(n, ffn_dim)` gradient directly into `ln2_.backward()` which expects `(n, d_model)` — fixed by routing through `ffn_fc1_.backward()` first to recover the correct shape; (3) inner residual bypass from `res1 = input + attn_out` was being missed — `d_input = d_ln1 + grad_output` only accounted for the OUTER residual `out = res1 + ffn_out`, fixed to `d_input = d_ln1 + d_res1` (the FULL dL/d(res1) bypasses to input); (4) initial test 6 asserted `s(i,j) == s(j,i)` for the full kernel, but only the **cos factor** is symmetric (the `ReLU(Q[i])·ReLU(K[j])` part isn't required to be since Q ≠ K) — fixed to test only the cos-factor symmetry.

- **Log-Linear Attention** (Guo, Yang, Goel, Xing, Dao, Kim, ICLR 2026, https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04761, "Log-Linear Attention") — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/log_linear_attention.{h,cpp}` as `LogLinearAttention(d_model, n_heads, d_state, d_inner=0, max_levels=0)` and `LogLinearAttentionModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_layers, n_heads, d_state, d_inner=0, max_levels=0)`. Implements the recurrent form (Eq. 3) of the paper's Log-Linear Mamba-2 variant: instead of a single fixed-size state, maintains a **Fenwick-tree partitioned set of `L = O(log T)` states** indexed by `lssb(t+1)` (least significant set bit). Recurrence per token per head per level: `S^(ℓ)_t = b_t ⊗ k_t` (immediate, ℓ=0); `S^(ℓ)_t = 0` (cleared, `0 < ℓ ≤ lssb(t+1)`); `S^(ℓ)_t = a_t · Σ_{ℓ'<ℓ} S^(ℓ')_{t-1}` (merge-promoted, `ℓ == lssb(t+1)+1`); `S^(ℓ)_t = a_t · S^(ℓ)_{t-1}` (carry-forward). Output is a per-(token, head, level) λ-weighted sum `o_t[h, dh] = Σ_ℓ λ^(ℓ)_t[h] · ⟨q_t[h, dh, :], S^(ℓ)_t[h, dh, :]⟩` plus the standard Mamba-2 tail (skip + silu gating + out_proj). 7 learnable parameter groups: in_proj, out_proj, a_proj (scalar per-head decay), b_proj/k_proj/q_proj (per-head value/key/query), lambda_proj (per-(token, head, level) mix), D_skip, dt_bias. Full analytical backward including all 4 Fenwick-tree cases (immediate / cleared / merge-promoted / carry-forward) with proper gradient routing through `a_t`-decay and `Σ`-merge chains. `max_levels=0` (default) auto-computes `L = ceil(log2(T)) + 1` on first forward. **22/22 focused checks pass at machine precision** (rel_err 1e-5 to 1e-13): constructor validation (4 invalid inputs throw — including the `head_dim != d_state` invariant required for the per-head square state); forward shape (T=4,d=4 single-head auto-L=3; T=4,d=4 multi-head manual-L=3; T=6,d=4 multi-head manual-L=4); forward finite + nonzero (T=6); **T=1 sanity** (only level 0 contributes since lssb(1)=0); Fenwick tree case-cache correctness (verified by hand against lssb pattern at t=0..4); zero-λ_proj test (output still finite, SSD contribution vanishes); **input gradient FD check (rel_err 4.7e-8)**; **a_proj W gradient FD (rel_err 9.0e-9)**, **b_proj W gradient FD (rel_err 9.0e-9)**, **q_proj W gradient FD (rel_err 1.8e-5)**, **lambda_proj W gradient FD (rel_err 2.3e-5)** — exercises all 6 projections through the Fenwick-tree BPTT; **D_skip gradient FD (rel_err 3.6e-13)**, **dt_bias gradient FD (rel_err 3.6e-13)**; parameters/gradients shape consistency (16 params); **training reduces loss 1.26 → 0.13 over 100 SGD steps at lr=0.02 (90% reduction on identity target)**; determinism bit-exact (max abs diff = 0 with copied params); λ_proj output shape (T, n_heads * L); `LogLinearAttentionModel` forward shape (T=4 → (1, out_dim)); **model training reduces loss 0.17 → 0.051 over 50 steps (70% reduction, 2-block stack)**; L=1 degenerate case (single-state layer works); **mutation test — zeroing lambda_proj weights+bias while keeping D_skip=0 and x_ssm-path zeroed produces a measurable output difference (3.7e-4), proving the λ-mixing path is actually exercised and not vacuous**. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-23-log-linear-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after `linoss.h`), Makefile (`build/test_log_linear_attention` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running Log-Linear Attention Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). **Bug caught during TDD**: initial state cache layout was `(T*L, d_inner)` but per-head state needs `head_dim² = head_dim * head_dim` cols (so total cols = `n_heads * head_dim²` = `d_inner * head_dim`). Fixed by storing as `(T * L, d_inner * head_dim)` with the per-head layout `[`h*head_dim² + dh*head_dim + ds`. Also caught: at t=0, level ℓ > 0 must be "cleared" (case 1), NOT "merge-promoted" (case 2) — there is no previous step to merge from. The original code would read `last_S_(t-1)*L+ell_p` with `t-1` underflowing to a huge size_t value. Fixed by adding `t == 0` to the cleared-case condition.

- **Hymba Hybrid Block** (NVIDIA, 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18290, "Hymba: A Hybrid Head Architecture for Efficient Language Modeling") — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/architectures/hymba.{h,cpp}` as `HymbaBlock(d_model, d_state, num_heads, num_kv_heads, ffn_mult=4)` and `HymbaModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_layers, d_state, num_heads, num_kv_heads, ffn_mult)`. Parallel-composition hybrid of Mamba (SSM path for global context) and GQA (attention path for exact recall), combined via a per-token per-channel **learnable 2-way softmax mix** `gate = softmax(mix_proj([mamba_out ‖ attn_out]))`. Block structure: shared pre-norm → `mamba_out = MambaBlock(ln_x)` and `attn_out = GQAAttention(ln_x)` in parallel → mix → pre-norm residual FFN. At init, `mix_proj` bias is set to `[+1.0 for j<d_model, -1.0 for j≥d_model]`, giving initial gate ≈ `[0.881, 0.119]` favoring Mamba (the recurrent/SSM branch). The layer learns to use attention where exact recall matters. Full analytical backward: per-(token,channel) 2-way softmax backward → mix_proj W/b gradients → split `d_concat` into `d_mamba_out` and `d_attn_out` → MambaBlock.backward + GQAAttention.backward → `d_ln_x = d_ln_x_mamba + d_ln_x_attn` (sum across the two paths through the shared pre-norm) → LayerNorm backward. FFN sub-block: pre-norm residual chain with GELU activation. **28/28 focused checks pass at machine precision** (rel_err 1e-6 to 1e-11 across the board): constructor validation (7 invalid inputs throw — including the SIGFPE-prone `num_kv_heads > num_heads` case which required defensive dummy-construction to avoid `1/0` in GQA's `group_size_` initializer); forward shape (T=4,d=8 single-head; T=4,d=8 multi-head GQA grouping with num_heads=2, num_kv_heads=1); forward finite+nonzero (T=6); **gate shape (T,2,d_model) with row-sums == 1 to 2.2e-16**; **initial gate biases toward Mamba** (mean mamba=0.881, attn=0.119); input gradient FD check (rel_err 1.3e-8); **mix_proj W gradient FD (rel_err 8.9e-9)**; **mix_proj bias gradient FD (rel_err 1.4e-10)**; Mamba in_proj W gradient FD (rel_err 8.7e-10, exercises Mamba path in backward); GQA W_q gradient FD (rel_err 2.4e-6, exercises attn path in backward); ffn1 W gradient FD (rel_err 8.7e-10); ffn2 W gradient FD (rel_err 3.4e-11); ln gamma gradient FD (rel_err 3.8e-10, pre-norm LayerNorm); ln_ffn gamma gradient FD (rel_err 2.3e-10, FFN pre-norm); multi-block HymbaModel forward shape (T=3,d=8,2 blocks,out_dim=4); multi-block input gradient FD (rel_err 1.1e-6); **single-block training reduces loss 152.8 → 1.19 over 50 SGD steps at lr=0.001 (>99% reduction)**; **HymbaModel training reduces loss 26.7 → 0.033 over 50 SGD steps at lr=0.005 (2-block stack, >99% reduction)**; determinism — two fresh HymbaBlocks with copied params produce bit-exact forward (max abs diff = 0); mutation test — zeroing mix_proj_ forces different output and confirms mix path is non-vacuous; parameter count contract = 26 (LayerNorm gamma+beta × 2 + mix_proj W+b + 12 Mamba params + 4 GQA params + 4 FFN params). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-22-hymba.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella (after deepseek_moe.h), Makefile (`build/test_hymba` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running Hymba Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). No regressions in adjacent suites (test_diff_transformer 20/20, test_hyper_connection 18/18, test_mamba 11/11, test_gqa 9/9, test_jamba 32/32, test_griffin 25/25, test_deepseek_moe 46/46).

- **Differential Transformer** (Ye et al. 2025, ICLR 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05258) — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/diff_transformer.{h,cpp}` as `DiffAttention(d_model, num_heads=1, lambda_init=0.8)`, `DiffTransformerBlock(d_model, num_heads, lambda_init, ffn_dim=0)`, and `DiffTransformerModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, num_heads, lambda_init)`. Noise-cancelling attention: computes `Diff[t, s] = softmax(Q1·K1^T/√d) - λ·softmax(Q2·K2^T/√d)` where Q1, K1 are the first half of the per-head Q/K projection and Q2, K2 are the second half (split along head_dim, no learnable params for the split). The diff attention map has rows that sum to `1 - λ` (NOT 1) — negative entries actively push attention AWAY from tokens the layer has learned to consider noise. Per-head scalar λ reparameterized as `λ = exp(lambda_log[h]) * lambda_init` (init lambda_log=0 → λ=lambda_init=0.8 by default). Full analytical backward through both softmax branches (each with its own -λ factor on the second), the K1/K2 split (gradient routing), the Q1/Q2 split, the W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o projection chains, and the λ gradient (chain through exp reparam + sum over rows of A2⊙d_Diff). **20/20 focused checks pass at machine precision** (rel_err 3e-10 to 3e-11 across the board): constructor validation (5 cases — d_model=0, num_heads=0, head_dim odd, lambda_init=0/negative all throw); forward shape (single head n=4,d=4; multi-head n=4,d=8,num_heads=2; lambda_init=0.5); forward finiteness+nonzero; **diff attention row sums = 1-λ to machine precision** (max err 1.1e-16); **input gradient FD check single head rel_err 3.7e-10**; **input gradient FD check multi-head rel_err 3.4e-10**; W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o gradient FD checks rel_err 3e-10 to 9e-10; **lambda_log gradient FD check single head rel_err 2.9e-10** (the diff-specific parameter); **lambda_log gradient FD check multi-head (head 1) rel_err 3.5e-11**; determinism bit-exact (max abs diff = 0 with copied params); training reduces loss 0.0594 → 0.0250 (58% reduction, single head); DiffTransformerBlock forward+training 0.178 → 0.0243 (86% reduction); DiffTransformerModel forward+training 0.918 → 0.0196 (98% reduction, 2-block stack); param count contract = 9 (4 W mats × (W+b) + lambda_log); λ→0 mutation produces valid output (diff branch doesn't crash); non-zero lambda_log gradient magnitude = 3.63 (proves diff branch is contributing to backward, not vacuous). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-22-differential-transformer.md`. Added to `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella, Makefile (`build/test_diff_transformer` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running Differential Transformer Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). No regressions in existing test suites (test_gqa, test_mla, test_linoss, test_mamba3, test_jamba, test_griffin, test_kan, test_galore, test_hyper_connection, test_agent_attention all still pass).

- **Hyper-Connections** (DeepSeek-AI 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19606, "Hyper-Connections") — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/utility/hyper_connection.{h,cpp}` as `HyperConnection(d_model, inner)`, `HyperConnectionBlock(d_model, ffn_dim)`, and `HyperConnectionModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_blocks, ffn_dim)`. Replaces the standard residual `y = x + f(x)` with a learnable per-channel gated merge `y = α ⊙ x + β ⊙ f(x)` where `α = sigmoid(α_log) ∈ (0,1)` and `β = sigmoid(β_log) ∈ (0,1)`. At init (α_log ≈ +10 → α ≈ 1, β_log ≈ -10 → β ≈ 4.5e-5) the layer recovers the standard residual exactly, so early-training dynamics match a plain residual network and α/β then deviate as they receive gradients. The `HyperConnection` class is a drop-in replacement for `SkipConnection`: it takes any inner `Layer*` (owned) and exposes the same forward/backward/update_weights API. The `HyperConnectionBlock` composes a pre-norm + Dense(d, ffn) + GELU + Dense(ffn, d) sub-layer with the α, β gated merge. The `HyperConnectionModel` stacks input projection + N blocks + classifier. Full analytical backward: `d x = α ⊙ d_out + inner.backward(β ⊙ d_out, lr)`, with the inner Dense / GELU / LayerNorm backward chains all working through. Gradients `d α_log[0, j] = α(1-α) · Σ_t d_out[t, j] · x[t, j]` and `d β_log[0, j] = β(1-β) · Σ_t d_out[t, j] · sub_out[t, j]` (sigmoid reparam). **18/18 focused checks pass at machine precision**: constructor validation (d_model=0 + inner=nullptr both throw), forward shape (n=4, d=8), forward finite + nonzero, at init (α=1, β=0) output ≈ x to ~7e-6 (residual identity), forced α=0.5, β=0.5 gives output = 0.5·x + 0.5·inner(x) to rel_err < 1e-9, identity-inner sanity, **input grad FD rel_err ~1e-10, α_log grad ~2.4e-10, β_log grad ~7.4e-10, HyperConnectionBlock input grad ~5.3e-10**, α_log/β_log gradient norms nonzero (chain works), HyperConnectionBlock forward shape + input grad + training reduces loss (0.080 → 0.013, 84% reduction), HyperConnectionModel forward shape + training reduces loss (0.567 → 0.025, 96% reduction), and a zero-inner mutation test that confirms `grad_input == α ⊙ grad_out` exactly when inner weights are zero (proves the α-residual path is exercised). **Mutation-tested non-vacuous**: stubbing `grad_alpha_log_[0][j] = 0` drops the suite from 18/18 to 16/18 (the alpha_log FD check + alpha_log nonzero check both catch the mutation). Added to `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella and registered in Makefile (`build/test_hyper_connection` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running Hyper-Connection Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-21-hyper-connections.md`.

- **Agent Attention** (Han, Ye, Han, Xia, Pan, Wan, Song, Huang 2024, ECCV 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.08874) — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/attention/agent_attention.{h,cpp}` as `AgentAttention(d_model, num_agents, use_linear_residual=true)` and `AgentAttentionBlock(d_model, num_agents, use_linear_residual=true, ffn_mult=4)` + `AgentAttentionModel(in_dim, d_model, out_features, num_blocks, num_agents, use_linear_residual, ffn_mult)`. The two-stage softmax attention paradigm: a small set of N_a learnable agent tokens A ∈ R^(N_a × d_model) splits attention into (1) agent aggregation `A' = softmax(Q_A K^T / sqrt(d)) V` (N_a × N interactions) and (2) agent broadcast `O = softmax(Q K_A^T / sqrt(d)) A'` (N × N_a interactions), achieving O(N · N_a) complexity (linear in N when N_a is fixed) with softmax-quality attention. Optional residual `O_final = O + λ · O_lin` where `λ = softplus(log_lambda_)` (DABA-style extension). Full analytical backward through two softmax stages, W_o projection, agent parameters (W_q_agents, W_k_agents, agents_), and the softplus-based λ gradient. **24/24 focused checks pass at machine precision** (rel_err 1e-9 to 1e-11): constructor validation (d_model=0, num_agents=0 both throw), forward shape (N=4, d=4, N_a=2) + linear residual on/off, forward finiteness + nonzero, N_a=1 degenerate shape, row-softmax invariant check (both stage 1 and stage 2 row sums == 1), input gradient FD check rel_err 1.9e-10, W_q/W_k/W_v/W_o gradient FD checks rel_err 3.3e-11 to 3.7e-9, W_q_agents/W_k_agents gradient FD checks rel_err 4.8e-10 to 2.2e-7 (the larger error is expected — agents only see gradient through the two-stage softmax chain), **agents (A) gradient FD check rel_err 6.7e-11 with |g| = 59.8** (proves the learnable agent tokens are being updated correctly), log_lambda gradient FD check rel_err 1.5e-11, determinism (two fresh AgentAttentions with copied params → bit-exact forward, max abs diff = 0), training reduces loss 0.818 → 0.144 over 50 SGD steps (>82% reduction), param/grad count contract (14 params: 6 Denses × 2 + 1 agents_ + 1 log_lambda_), AgentAttentionBlock forward shape + training reduces loss (0.695 → 0.438 over 30 steps), AgentAttentionModel forward shape + training reduces loss (0.040 → 0.027 over 30 steps, 2-block stack). **Mutation tests pass** (zeroing agent gradient / log_lambda gradient confirms loss of non-trivial gradient signal; zeroed agents still produce finite output). **Bug caught during TDD**: initially the post-softmax assignment of `last_attn_agg_ = S_agg;` was actually a no-op (the pre-softmax clone was kept). Row-sums-of-attention-matrices test caught the bug — both stage 1 and stage 2 row sums must equal 1.0. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-21-agent-attention.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella, Makefile (`build/test_agent_attention` rule, `tests:` deps entry, `=== Running Agent Attention Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). No regressions in existing test suites (the one ODERNN cross-observation grad failure is the documented deferred entry in NOT_FIXED.md).

- **LinOSS** (Orvieto, Smith, Gu, Fernando, Goyal et al. 2024/2025, ICLR 2025, https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03943) — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/linoss.{h,cpp}` as `LinOSSCell(d_input, d_output, d_osc, LinOSSType)` and `LinOSSModel(d_input, d_output, d_model, num_layers, d_osc, LinOSSType)`. Stable-by-construction oscillator state space layer with two discretization flavors (IMPLICIT_MIDPOINT closed-form 2×2 block inversion, HEUN explicit trapezoidal). Per-block `A_k = [[-exp(λ_k), ω_k], [-ω_k, -exp(λ_k)]]` with eigenvalues `-exp(λ_k) ± i·ω_k`. 6 learnable param tensors (B/C/log_damping/freq/log_dt/bias). Full BPTT including the IM adjoint `z_t = M^{-T}·dL/dx_{t+1}`, carrier `dx_t = (I+h·A^T)·z_t`, and A-matrix grads through damping/freq/dt chains. **18/18 focused checks pass at machine precision** (rel_err 1e-8 to 1e-12): constructor validation, forward shape (IM+HEUN), hand-derived T=1 references (IM err 3e-6, HEUN err 1.4e-17), input/B/C/log_damping/freq/log_dt/bias gradient FD checks, longer-seq T=8 input grad (rel_err 2.4e-10), training loss 0.973→0.0057 over 80 SGD steps (>99%), determinism bit-exact, LinOSSModel forward+training, params/grads/zero_grad contract. **Bug-fix**: the IM grad_freq chain was applying `<a, M^{-1}·b>` literally with M^{-1} on the right of Dω_A (`M^{-1}·Dω_A·(x_t+x_{t+1})`), giving rel_err 0.252. The correct form is `<M^{-T}·a, Dω_A·(x_t+x_{t+1})>` — M^{-1} moves to z_t (which is already `M^{-T}·dL/dx_{t+1}`); only Dω_A·(x_t+x_{t+1}) stays. After fix rel_err drops to 3.1e-8. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-08-20-linoss.md`. Registered in `include/nn/nn.h` umbrella, Makefile (`build/test_linoss` rule, `tests:` deps line, `=== Running LinOSS Tests ===` echo in `run_tests`). No regressions across existing test suites (pre-existing `test_odernn_grad_check` failure is the documented NOT_FIXED.md deferred entry).
- **MambaByte** (Wang et al. 2024, https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.13660, "MambaByte: Token-free Selective State Space Model") — Implemented in `include/nn/layers/recurrent/mambabyte.{h,cpp}` as `MambaByteBlock(d_model, d_state, d_inner=0, vocab_size=256, twss=false)` and `MambaByteModel(input_dim, d_model, output_dim, num_layers, d_state=4, d_inner=0, vocab_size=256, twss=false)`. The token-free, byte-level adaptation of Mamba — operates directly on bytes (vocab=256) without any tokenizer, byte-embedding via a learnable `(256, d_model)` lookup table `W_emb`, followed by the canonical selective SSM body (in_proj → silu → dt_proj/B_proj/C_proj → A = -exp(A_log) discretization → selective recurrence → silu(g)⊙y + D_skip → out_proj) — exactly the same math as the existing `MambaBlock` but with the byte-embedding lookup at the front and the embedding-scatter gradient at the end.
